        variant  mean_still_bad_count  mean_max_variance_ratio_after  mean_emg_band_power_after  mean_eeg_band_retention  mean_interchannel_corr  mean_ica_excluded  mean_autoreject_reject_fraction  mean_runtime_s

A2_ica_then_car              0.666667                       3.856625               1.090904e-13                 0.170999                0.379614           2.166667                           0.1010      247.133333

--- How to read this ---
mean_still_bad_count            : LOWER is better (channels still >4x median
                                   variance after cleaning).
mean_max_variance_ratio_after    : LOWER is better, closer to 1-3 is healthy.
mean_emg_band_power_after        : LOWER is better (less residual muscle noise),
                                   check against eeg_band_retention for over-cleaning.
mean_eeg_band_retention          : should stay close between A1/A2 -- large gap =
                                   one ordering is destroying more real EEG signal.
mean_interchannel_corr           : this is the ordering-specific check. If ICA runs
                                   BEFORE CAR (A2), CAR afterwards re-introduces a
                                   common signal that ICA never saw/could remove --
                                   watch for A2 showing unusually different (often
                                   higher, less physiologically structured) correlation
                                   than A1.
mean_autoreject_reject_fraction  : LOWER is better -- fraction of 3s pseudo-epochs
                                   AutoReject would still flag as bad after cleaning.

--- Automatic note (heuristic, please confirm visually) ---
A1_car_then_ica favoured by: no metric
A2_ica_then_car favoured by: ['lower residual EMG-band power', 'fewer epochs still flagged bad by AutoReject']
